# Remaining Mavelli reference audit

Every Git content match of the four required final search patterns is listed below. Audit documents themselves are excluded from recursive self-listing.
A=frozen historical source; B=frozen historical output; C=provenance/audit; D=explicit legacy comparison; E=migration record.
No unclassified reference remains. Current authority,CI/config/preflight/model card/registry target PNAS2017_full_reference.
The original171-file inventory is a before-edit audit; newly created legacy copies and migration files are covered here.

| docs/theory/reduction_certificate.md:6 | D | Registered legacy comparison/tool; preserved in place and excluded from active defaults | **Input model:** frozen B1 Mavelli literature-reference backbone at commit `c2bb0ae63ba106d4f51bf2182dfd28dfe187e743`. The canonical model definition, parameters, generated RHS, and D1–D6 science are unchanged. D7 preregistration and acceptance criteria entered history in commit `cec84861ab829bf451d6902283e5f078a6e89401`, before formal validation. |
| docs/theory/reduction_certificate.md:18 | D | Registered legacy comparison/tool; preserved in place and excluded from active defaults | Mavelli §2.2 describes aminoacyl-tRNA synthetases, ATP consumption, AMP and PPi production, while the backbone pools energy species into generic NTP/NXP and describes RS using apparent Michaelis-Menten factors. This D7 construction is a **model extension for reduction analysis**, not a derivation of the canonical apparent rate law from a unique microscopic mechanism. |
| docs/theory/reduction_certificate.md:36 | D | Registered legacy comparison/tool; preserved in place and excluded from active defaults | The notation \(NTP_{RS}\), when used, means this same canonical NTP coordinate. The ATP-to-NTP mapping is bookkeeping for the coarse-grained Mavelli backbone; it does not claim that all NTP species are ATP. With frozen multiplicities, \(f_{TA}=46/20=2.3\), but every runtime calculation derives the factor from `p.n_T/p.n_A` rather than hardcoding the decimal. |
| docs/theory/reduction_certificate.md:63 | D | Registered legacy comparison/tool; preserved in place and excluded from active defaults | All non-RS rates \(V_{TX},V_{nt,deg},V_{TL},V_{TL,deg},V_{EN}\) are obtained by calling `rhs_pure_literature_reference`. Their rate laws are not copied into the extension. Canonical \(V_{RS}\) is retained only for reference/provenance and never contributes to a D7 state update. Returned rate slot 3 contains actual transfer \(v_2\). |
| docs/theory/reduction_certificate.md:118 | D | Registered legacy comparison/tool; preserved in place and excluded from active defaults | **This QSSA rate is not algebraically identical to Mavelli Eq. (8).** It is the reduced law of the explicitly declared one-intermediate surrogate. Local matching below does not establish global equality to the product of three apparent Michaelis-Menten factors. |
| docs/theory/reduction_certificate.md:122 | D | Registered legacy comparison/tool; preserved in place and excluded from active defaults | The frozen Mavelli model supplies apparent RS kinetics and the average catalyst concentration; these do not uniquely identify microscopic constants for this specific lumped two-step mechanism. No unrelated aaRS microscopic constants or apparent Michaelis constants are reinterpreted as such a source. The D7 micro-parameters are labeled **`synthetic_reduction_only`** in [the machine-readable parameter record](../../models/reductions/rs_qssa/parameters.json), without altering the canonical parameter file. |

| references/R01_Mavelli2015/A_Simple_Protein_Synthesis_Model_PURE.md:6 | A | Frozen historical source/implementation | ## Fabio Mavelli |
| references/R01_Mavelli2015/A_Simple_Protein_Synthesis_Model_PURE.md:14 | A | Frozen historical source/implementation | B Pasquale Stano pasquale.stano@uniroma3.it Fabio Mavelli fabio.mavelli@uniba.it Roberto Marangoni roberto.marangoni@unipi.it |
| references/R01_Mavelli2015/A_Simple_Protein_Synthesis_Model_PURE.md:19 | A | Frozen historical source/implementation | F. Mavelli et al. |
| references/R01_Mavelli2015/A_Simple_Protein_Synthesis_Model_PURE.md:40 | A | Frozen historical source/implementation | F. Mavelli et al. |
| references/R01_Mavelli2015/A_Simple_Protein_Synthesis_Model_PURE.md:41 | A | Frozen historical source/implementation | of synthetic cells and, in particular to TX–TL systems, has only been marginal. In particular, we and others have only very recently started to face this issue, by adopting different approaches of very different complexity, from basic coarse-grained models (Sunami et al. 2010; Karzbrun et al. 2011; Stögbauer et al. 2012) to more detailed and stochastic ones (Frazier et al. 2009; Lazzerini-Ospri et al. 2012; Calviello et al. 2013). In this paper, we present a non-stochastic model for TX–TL systems that has been specifically designed to be a companion for experiments carried out with the PURE system kit, providing a simple tool for the mathematical modeling of protein synthe- sis. This is a model that has been primarily designed for conditions where intrinsic stochastic effects—those due to small number of molecules—are negligible. With- out neglecting the importance (and the accuracy) of stochastic models, especially for micro-compartmentalized reactions such as those of interest in synthetic cell research (Mavelli et al. 2014), our model has the advantage of being simple and nevertheless able to capture most of the essential aspects of TX–TL reactions. In particular, the model mirrors the four PURE system modules (Shimizu et al. 2005), namely transcription, translation, amino acid–tRNA charging and energy regeneration. We are aimed at designing a model able to describe the macroscopic, measurable output of PURE system, on the bases of its most important, controllable variables, avoiding a finer detailed level, where most of the reactions kinetic or thermodynamic constants are unknown (or not measurable) and not controllable. |

| results/baselines/b1_mavelli2015/audit/hardening_20260918/redundancy_evidence.json:3 | B | Frozen historical output/source-data evidence |   "explanation": "The provenance-bound rerun b1_fig4_hardening_20260918 (commit 10235db, branch audit/b1-hardening) repeated the B1 Fig.4 benchmark of the legacy AI baseline (results/literature_reference, now results/baselines/b1_mavelli2015). Before deleting its trajectory/rates CSVs and figures, byte identity against the baseline was verified.
